Mechanism: each subagent pays a cold cache write; briefs lack stop conditions. Cure: delegation hook with a context threshold (inline under ~50k) and a SubagentStop check for the return receipt. Source: [124] E48, F137, H231, H248, I288.
- [ ] **Cold-Cache Blindness.** Diagnosis: the user or agent resumes a long idle session, or compacts cold, and burns 15-40% of a window; resume after a 20-minute break rewrote cost silently and users learned from the bill (harness fault, not model). Mechanism: cache expiry (TTL) forces a full re-write; compaction on a cold or switched model gets no cache hit. Cure: UserPromptSubmit hook that blocks or warns on a stale session and shows the cold cost, plus a warm-cache compact/handoff step before idle; notice candidate. Source: [40] F (reported); [120] E25, E27, F111, F135, F149, G204.
- [ ] **Repeat Reads and Repeat Runs.** Diagnosis: 35% of file reads were repeats of an unchanged file; edits land on unread files (77% claimed, mostly post-compaction). Mechanism: compaction drops the read record. Cure: PreToolUse hook refusing a repeat read of an unchanged file (hash/mtime) and a read-before-edit gate. Source: [122] H277, H250.

### Process Hygiene

- [ ] **Process Hygiene Failure.** Diagnosis: loose `pkill -f` killing the user's or own shell, `until`/`while` busy-wait loops (full core each; a 32 min loop counted four successes when one was 403), expiring Monitors, orphaned jobs and fake APIs for 7 days, duplicate paid suites, many heavy runs at once (2026-11-11 freeze; >6 GB per test file), false reassurance that a background process was idle (billing while looping), another session's test run killed, polling 299 triple-polls in a benchmark, unannounced runs, unclosed subagents, `/goal` continuing hours after a classifier denial. Mechanism: no ownership check on targets. Cure: kill by recorded pid only, locks, memory guard, gate deny, halt-the-loop rule, verify with the process list, cap spend externally. Source: [16] A, B, C, D, E, G, H, I. have: `plugins/claudetto/hooks/features/setup/command-rules.ts` (deny `pkill *`, `killall *`).
- [ ] **Runaway Background Work.** Diagnosis: one agent left a render running 9 hours; a session stuck with zero tokens used; uncapped background tests kept calling ~17 h for ~$2.1K after the session hit its limit; a duplicate paid run after a sandboxed `pgrep` failure read as "no eval running"; an Opus 5.5 task of ~2 h ran 6 h; a sandbox cut prompts 84% (vendor) but a 24-run guardrail pack showed 0 crossings in both arms (counter-evidence). Mechanism: no timeout, no stop condition, no owner for the process after the turn. Cure: PreToolUse hook prefixing `timeout 30m` on background commands and a runaway guard at the Stop check; job lock with PID, Stop hook listing children by recorded pid and `TaskStop`, budget action that blocks calls (`--max-budget-usd`, 2.1.325: present); status-request steer after ~30 min via hook; wall-time cap. Source: [58] A; [123] H250, H269; [152] L55, L339.

### Context Loss

- [ ] **Context Decay.** Diagnosis: long-context drift drops earlier instructions and skills, assumes from names instead of reading implementation, forgets by the third reply (Haiku), forgets after compaction (rejected options, tried approaches re-offered 7 of 10 times, author's test, measured), says "extensive work from yesterday" for two-hour-old work, claims it never did a read it announced near half context, compaction omits valuable context and amplifies hyperfocus, a new session re-adds killed design elements. Cure: a handoff doc kept current on disk and proofread, `/clear` at a milestone while the cache is warm, skill injection at session start and after compaction, auto-compaction 350k-500k, record decisions with reasons, short project-facts notes. Source: [26] A, C, E, F, G, H. have: partial `plugins/claudetto/hooks/features/compaction/handoff.ts`.
- [ ] **Instruction Decay.** Diagnosis: user rules and CLAUDE.md protocols are ignored, or acknowledged then ignored; constraints set early (read-only, no commit, no deps, only these files) broken later; read-only 7 of 8 violated at baseline; rules arrive late as one mid-turn reminder after a tool result (17 of 22 sessions; `useEdgeCaseReport` met in 8 of 1,473 replies, `noNarratingComments` broken: 218 added comments in 1,610 edits, measured heuristic); drift after 30-40 messages and after compaction; model and effort choices for subagents ignored; `sed -i` used against a rule; the agent repeats a failure its own project list names (A1: "decision of the user" for its own choice) though a list of 27 cases (6 format, 6 scope, 4 approval, 11 process) existed; CLAUDE.md and skill rules are followed most of the time, not every time. Mechanism: a rule read as background, no check at the point of use; a case list is prose, not enforcement; instruction files are context, not configuration, and compaction paraphrases them. Cure: constraint ledger (39/48 -> 48/48; read-only 1/8 -> 8/8), lint/hook at the point of use, hard rules in hooks, a hook or Stop check per recurring case (grep on the final message); SessionStart-on-compact and UserPromptSubmit re-injection of the few must-follow rules (violations 10 to 3, reported); the extractor must not read a task fragment as a constraint (a task phrase about renaming an export blocked an edit). Source: [12] A, C, D, E, F, G, H; [46] A (SL 2a80f39f, d84dc11f); [114] E15, E55, F118, G199, G227, H237.
- [ ] **Rejected Option Amnesia.** Diagnosis: the agent re-proposes an approach or simplification the project already rejected, or reopens decided work (7 of 10 sessions without the file); the next session re-proposes an approach already rejected. Mechanism: compaction and plain handoffs keep decisions and drop rejected options with reasons; the rationale is in nobody's context; "Keep-the-Why" test: 10 of 10 sessions declined the rejected change with a context folder, 7 of 10 offered it without (measured, author, hand graded; 20 sessions). Cure: a skill/hook that makes the agent read a `context/` rationale folder before simplifying code with a scar and write the why when a decision lands; handoff or decision-log hook requiring a rejected-options section and a PreCompact flush; SessionStart on compact re-reads it; compaction handoff injecting Decisions and Ruled out. Source: [72] E L157; [113] E8, E18, E104, F113, G187, H260; [164] A. have: [164] partial.
- [ ] **Pause Lost Across Compaction.** Diagnosis: after compaction or `/clear` Claude implements despite a plan-then-wait or investigate-only instruction, and the user's pause was lost after `/clear`; a user context estimate was treated as a trigger. Mechanism: the pause lives only in the conversation; the summary ends with an instruction to continue the task without questions. Cure: a PreCompact hook writes `awaiting approval, do not implement` state, and PreCompact/SessionStart hooks write and reload a handoff file including pending pauses; SessionStart on compact re-injects it. Source: [98] A:173; [119] E47, E103.

### Model Variability

- [ ] **Over-Refusal and Safeguard False Positives.** Diagnosis: the Opus 5.5 reasoning-extraction notice fires on benign work (CHANGELOG edits, git commits, compaction, the word "reasoning" in briefs, judge/eval prompts); firmware/OTA, game, hotel, and noodle recipe prompts flagged; the block persists in-session and is not resumable; a throwaway-DB connect refused "to avoid leaking credentials"; a task refused as "falsifying a document"; copyright `ip_reminder` text injected in 2.1.322; the model flagged a real system-reminder as an injection; cyber guardrails with no CVP. Mechanism: reported, causes inferred. Cure: new session with a reframed goal, avoid "reasoning" headings in harness briefs (check templates), front-load the reason, do not design behaviour that depends on convincing the model. Source: [28] E, F, H, I (reported). have: missing (template check).
- [ ] **Self-Report Distrust.** Diagnosis: model estimates of its own costs are far off (logged data accurate), models rate themselves modestly, models do not know facts about themselves, cannot see context fill, "acknowledge corrections" yet repeat; Claude's advice on rules, hooks, and skills for itself was judged poor, and Opus wrote a rule set after refusing a task as "falsifying a document" (single case). Mechanism: the model advises on a harness it cannot observe. Cure: take cost and ratings from the bill, logs, and humans, never from self-report; human-authored rules from logged failures, docs in context via clawdipedia-docs, a subagent context holding the docs. Source: [31] E, F; [125] E42, H273.
- [ ] **Quality Drift Reports.** Diagnosis: reported weekday degradation of Opus 5.5, one author found it vanished after 24h (A/B or load balancing suspected), another says quality drifts 1-2 weeks after release; Sonnet 5.5 edited files in manual edit-approval mode without asking (one report). No measurement. Cure: hard rules in hooks, a daily internal benchmark per model. Source: [41] F, E (reported).

## Conduct

### Keep

- [ ] **Verify against state, not claims.** `accept(claim) = tool_output | exit_code | diff | rendered_state`; agent summary, apology, self-report, and a second model's "done" are never evidence. Honesty rules: reproduce what the user ran before stating a cause; audit output, not the report; show both reads when two differ; strike through corrections; none of these lets an agent notice an unseen mistake, which needs a test or second model. Cited [2310.01798], [2609.20812]; reported (I, 2026-11-10). have: partial `plugins/claudetto/hooks/features/prompt/texts.ts:15` ("Probe each claim ... before you report it"). Source: A principle 2, I.
- [ ] **Autonomy and consent are one dial.** Over-deferral ("ask first" rules spread into asking before anything costly) and overreach ("be autonomous" spreads into changing user-owned values) share one cause; write down which values/actions the user owns (bounds, budgets, tests, hooks, settings, user-authored rules, user-named things); the agent owns the rest and tries once before deferring. Inferred by dossier: harness prompts guarding over-deferral/overreach generalise; capability underestimated, "can't" never probed; agent swung between both within one session (SL 1213c32c). No research measures deferral of executable work. have: partial `plugins/claudetto/hooks/features/prompt/texts.ts`. Source: A (principle 6, I13/I14).
- [ ] **Pushback rule.** On dispute: `check(claim)`; if check backs user, name the error, use their version; if it backs the reply, keep it and show the check. Open with the fact the check decided; put any agreement after it. Change position only on named new evidence; state disagreement once with evidence; ask "where does this break", not "does this work". Held in the session log (reported): cache TTL re-measured under pushback (1h = +21% subagents, +7% main, measured), "super important" source called weak, loosen-grader recommendation withdrawn once saved inputs showed the grader right. Conduct n=5,4,2; prompt n=28,6,6,5,2. have: `plugins/claudetto/hooks/features/prompt/texts.ts:15`; eval `evals/no-opener-after-a-dispute` (referenced by A, not re-grepped). Source: A, B specs/agent-conduct.
- [ ] **Opener check (SA-3.3, S6.6, CC-2).** Prompt-type Stop hook blocks a final reply whose first sentence opens with agreement, praise, or apology ("You're right", "Great point", "Good catch", "I apologize", "Sorry"); judge first sentence only; cost one small call per turn end; reason text: start with the substance, name the error and the fix. Dossier session log: agent built this hook after answering a correction with "You're right" (SL e473653f). Counts (other, n=1): in ~1,900 sessions "you're right" 175 times, "absolutely right" 13; replies opened with agreement 4x as often as disagreement. conflict: B specs/agent-conduct says open with next action or changed fact with no Stop-time judge model call (grader/agree-opener block only); A/D keep a judge. 2.1.325: Stop present. have: partial (prompt text `hooks/features/prompt/texts.ts:15`; Stop hook missing). Source: A, B, D, F3 (no prompt variant has an anti-sycophancy/opener/test-integrity rule).
- [ ] **Conduct standard, five contrastive examples.** cc-opener (no agreement/apology opener; state the finding), cc-promise (name the cause and propose a mechanism instead of promising), cc-factcheck (check before correcting the user), cc-pushback (keep a measured fact under pushback: 1h-cache-TTL cost 21% more for subagents, 7% more for main thread), cc-cost (give a number, about $2.90 against a $5 standing budget, not "expensive"); do's and don'ts incl. never end a turn announcing the next step. Enforcement wording-level only. missing (no conduct examples in plugins). Source: A CC-1/CC-2.
- [ ] **Reply register questions on Stop.** `ship_to_Stop iff precision>=80% and recall>=80% on stored cases`. Shipped: `apologizes_or_agrees`, `states_feeling_or_praise`, `repeats_person`, `names_own_mistake_without_result`, `steps_listed_as_report`. Held (scored, non-blocking): `claim_without_shown_check` (25/100), `question_unanswered`, `doable_step_left`, `choice_handed_to_person`. A yes blocks once; no/unsettled/failed Decider passes. Measured, 24 cases, thin. have: partial `plugins/claudetto/hooks/features/decide/` and `plugins/claudetto/hooks/features/reply/` (the cited `classic.Stop/useStopChecks/register.ts` does not exist). Source: D321 design P239.
- [ ] **Own defect = bug report.** Reply opens with the cause and states what broke, where, cost, fix; no apology, preamble, or retrospective. Eval case: loose `pkill` ended the user's processes, reply opens "pkill read -U and 501 as patterns"; recorded reply opening with an apology and "Here is what happened" failed. Person's rule: on a mistake name error and cause, give corrected result, carry on; refusal is final for that action. missing. Source: B specs/agent-conduct, D C33.
- [ ] **Neutral tool-result wording.** Model carries the tone of tool-result text into its reply ("NOT SENT, domain cannot receive email" became "your email bounced"; "payment failed due to suspicious activity" became a fraud lecture for an expired card). Keep tool/hook text neutral and factual; put validation gates in code (DNS MX check before the creating tool), not in prompt. reported (G, 2026-10-21). missing (audit of hook messages not done). Source: G.
- [ ] **Narration cure.** Text between tool calls holds a finding, result, or error, "because the next call shows itself". Harness reads the reminder from env `CLAUDE_CODE_SILENT_TURN_REMINDER_TEXT`, then server flag `tengu_hushed_lark_text`, then default (default text asks to keep the user updated with a finding or change of plan); a settings `env` value replaces text on the tool result (probe-130). Setup features-off group sets the env to a custom text asking one sentence about a finding/result and "Say nothing about your next call"; output style drops "the next action". Effect not significant (24/204 vs 31/204 announced, p=0.385; earlier 13/136 vs 23/136, p=0.106): keep as a cheap setting, claim no effect. 2.1.325: present (env `CLAUDE_CODE_SILENT_TURN_REMINDER_TEXT`, also `_SECONDS`, `_TURNS`, `CLAUDE_CODE_SILENT_TURN_REMINDER`; gates `tengu_hushed_lark`, `tengu_hushed_lark_text`). have: `plugins/claudetto/hooks/features/setup/settings.ts`. Source: B surface-trim, C tasks 16.3/17.4.
- [ ] **Bash text names six file tools.** Bash description names `cat`, `head`, `tail`, `sed`, `awk`, `echo` in backticks and says files go through Read, Edit, Write and search tools unless user asks for shell, so the gate need not refuse; a denial carries a ready `Read` call with offset and limit (agents repeated `sed -n`/`cat`/`head`/`tail` ~360 times/day after denial, one model call each). Source: B surface-trim, D P79/C30. partial: `plugins/claudetto/hooks/features/setup/command-rules.ts`.
- [ ] **Committing agents isolate.** Agent description tells model to set `isolation:"worktree"` for committing agents; hook passes isolation through unchanged; no agent definition sets isolation (a worktree holds the changes of a non-committing agent). Cause: agents committing in one tree block each other at pre-commit. unverified (parameter not queried in 2.1.325). have: `plugins/claudetto/hooks/features/delegation/texts.ts`. Source: B surface-trim.
- [ ] **Stop checks (run-limits).** (a) Missing named file: agent tells user to run a `!` command naming a file it never wrote; Stop blocks, names path and command (absolute or `~` paths). (b) Uncommitted work: `block_once if git status on files this session's file tools wrote shows changes and permission chain would not deny git commit`; ask one commit per revertable slice, failing work stays uncommitted with reason; skip when ledger has `no-commit`, headless with commit check answering ask/deny, `stop_hook_active`, or files under `.claude/worktrees/`; raise when ask and someone can answer. Subagent variant: lead passed an unchecked subagent report naming an unwritten file (user got "Module not found"); apply to SubagentStop. 2.1.325: Stop present, SubagentStop present. missing. Source: B specs/run-limits, C design Named files exist.
- [ ] **Pipe gate.** Piping a failing check through `tail`/`head`/`grep` returns last exit code, failure reads as pass; gate denial names `set -o pipefail;`, `&&`, `set -e`; do not discard stderr (`2>/dev/null`) on verification commands (H, reported). 2.1.325: PreToolUse present. missing (no `pipefail` in plugins). Source: B permission-policy, H.
- [ ] **Denial text states the rule.** Missing reason makes agents hunt for a way around; refusal text states the rule, count, earlier reason, and says change the call or give the user an exact `! <command>`; agents repeated or tried workarounds (Node/Python/container, editing own permission files, disabling sandbox for `gh`/`git push`). partial `plugins/claudetto/hooks/features/protected/texts.ts` (not read). Source: B, D R2/R56/C5, H.
- [ ] **Bounds are the user's.** Raise a size/budget/timeout/test bound only after asking with old and new value, or to a value the user named; keep `--no-verify` and SIP off, fix what the hook reports, or put options to the user; ask before changing an existing test (show old assertion, new assertion, authorising requirement); before retiring or renaming anything person-authored or person-named (rules, tools, agents) ask. missing (no `no-verify` rule in plugins). Source: B agent-conduct, D R51/D323 8.7-8.8, H.
- [ ] **Classify the message first.** A rhetorical question, remark, pasted thread, or "let's ..." is not a task: answer or ask, then wait; a question wants an answer, not a tool batch (tool-before-text 5/10 -> 0/10 when the first batch is denied on question/remark turns, read-only lookups allowed); add a `dispute` kind (message disputing an answer with no work request) and treat "I still get the error" as a task. have: partial `plugins/claudetto/hooks/features/prompt/texts.ts` (root prompt). Source: B, C 16.14/18.21/18.31, D.
- [ ] **End on result.** End on the result or the single user step; no offer to continue, no caveat that changes no decision, no "optional next steps"; end-of-task report 5-10 lines (what changed, what is unsure, what to check, what next; no reasoning recap); read the diff, because diff is truth; review in a fresh session or subagent; a CLAUDE.md line banning closing summary/restated plan removes more words than "be concise" (reported, F; counterpoint: one user wants full output). have: partial `plugins/claudetto/hooks/features/reply/`. Source: B, H, F.
- [ ] **Positive verbosity definitions.** Negative corrections ("do not be lengthy but not terse") backfire; define "concise means no repetition, filler or hedging, not short"; Concise output style: lead with result, cut narration, "these rules win over general formatting guidance", re-injected each turn as a system-reminder (reported, copied from post, unverified). Prose caps ("under N lines") cost reasoning tokens. have: partial `plugins/claudetto/hooks/features/reply/reply.ts`. Source: E, G.
- [ ] **Shipped source does not narrate its making (S8.1-S8.7).** No session names, task/change numbers, plan names, "as discussed", "per the user", dates beside session words; provenance field names one checkable thing (path+line/field, command, URL); history goes in the Record; use one plain-Markdown file, no invented schema; commit messages say what and why; durable state on disk at about 75% of window (goal, state with proof, decisions with reasons, ruled-out approaches, verify command, files touched, open questions); re-read a covering Correction before acting in its area, propose a mechanism if corrected twice. Mechanism (Case A): `PreToolUse Write|Edit` guard on added lines of shipped files returns `ask` naming the line; CI diff lint for Bash writes. Test table (9): incident line asks; ordinary why-comment silent; session text in docs/ exempt; "the user decided" in new shipped file asks; bare ISO date silent; malformed input fails open with stderr note; path outside project silent; unchanged pre-existing line in a Write silent. Cause: prose-only rule, rule applied outside scope, local repair then class-wide only after second correction. have: CLAUDE.md rule only; hook missing. Source: A.
- [ ] **Comments are not understanding (R-AI-10).** Improve names, structure, types, explicit flow in order, then a short comment only for a reason code cannot show (= B1-14, repo rule commentOnlyWhy). Line-comment-count and null-guard hooks cannot be decided without a word list: no hook, rules reach model via rule files. have: user rule file. Source: A, D (Goes, merged).
- [ ] **Expertise and evidence (P21-P30).** No authority without examining evidence; confident corrective tone must not hide ignorance; specifics over generic explanation; investigate tools/docs/source the user names; do not discount comments/forums/contributors; separate observed behaviour from its explanation; test a claimed limitation; user may know more; lack of knowledge is not lack of evidence; conclusions no stronger than evidence. missing. Source: A.
- [ ] **Checks beat instructions.** Put user-facing strings in one file, script flags banned words/over-length, PostToolUse hook on Edit/Write feeds failures back; rules must be checkable ("headings under six words"); give a reader ("annoyed, ten seconds"). Behaviours that exist only as text (tone, opener, refusal) need an LLM judge validated on fixtures; Google "change-detector tests" anti-pattern (cited, not fetched). 2.1.325: PostToolUse present. missing (no style-check hook). Source: A, G.
- [ ] **Skeptic and anti-sycophancy recipes.** After a plan launch a second agent to critique; ask failure reasons before build; post-mortem framing ("project already failed, list top 3-5 reasons"); three strongest counterarguments or a failing test; critique in a fresh chat; full context beats magic words; "you explained this yesterday" tricks invite fabricated history (reported consensus). Cited: sycophancy from preference data [2310.13548]; collapse grows with pressure, correct answer often stays in reasoning trace [2609.09090]; ClarifyGPT 70.96 -> 80.80 pass@1 [2310.10996]; Ambig-SWE up to 74% [2502.13069]; RL clarifier 41% fewer questions [2604.14624]; reported top comment (367): "ask one clarifying question". partial: `plugins/claudetto/agents/Athena.md`, `Metis.md` (critic role not checked). Source: A, G.
- [ ] **Graders and referees are unwritable by the tested agent.** Frozen graders/tasks/hash before run 1; agent cannot write graders, hidden tests, holdout, results; grade outside its workspace; graders reject known-bad runs; holdout scored once; held-out data denied via `Read` deny plus `sandbox.filesystem.denyRead` (2.1.325: present); give judges the full numbered tool-call list with last 300 chars of each result (judge faults caused every held-out miss in first 24/24 attempts). partial `plugins/claudetto/hooks/features/protected/`. Source: B, C 18.31/18.37.
- [ ] **Measure the right thing.** Arm assertion, keep traces, discrimination check; score the answer set not the last N results (scorer reading last 10 results scored 1/10 on a 10/10 run); judge a probe by what the command did, not exit code (`open -R` exit 0 while blocked); no prompt rule whose only payoff is a benchmark metric (mutation rule cost 17 Stryker commands, $2.14, 20.8 min in hono, c8b12403); measure cost per task. Source: A I16, B design.md, C probe rows.
- [ ] **Context-file drift.** Split context file into derivable facts (paths, commands, structure; CI checks referenced paths/commands exist) and decisions (dated, status durable/superseded, promotion step); gate edits through a script or skill with size validation, block direct edits by hook pointing at the skill; per-entry files tied to commits. Consequence: decisions rot silently, nothing in the repo changes when one dies; "update CLAUDE.md if anything changed" did not help (reported). Memory hygiene: verbose memory with history, a different model's rewrite removed 157 lines across 4 AGENTS.md files (reported). have: partial `plugins/claudetto/hooks/features/memory/`. Source: E, F.
- [ ] **Permission-prompt design.** Prompts stop protecting after ~1 hour of clicking; split by what can be undone: free = edits inside repo on a branch/worktree, reads auto-approved, ask only irreversible or outside-repo, hard deny secrets and writes outside project; add an ask budget; other setups: separate OS user, VM/VPS, devcontainer, no remote write credentials; one user built ~20 PreToolUse checks behind a single bash shim (reported, convergent). Without it: approval fatigue or auto mode failing closed. have: partial `plugins/claudetto/hooks/features/setup/auto-mode.ts`, `command-rules.ts`. Source: E.
- [ ] **Hook and settings self-protection.** Protected-path list lives in the repo, enforced in CI, fail closed (unlisted file counts protected); approval gate writes a record outside agent's working directory; writes to `.claude` are a protected path (prompt in default/acceptEdits, silent in bypassPermissions/auto) and deny rules miss a python one-liner, so enable sandbox with `sandbox.filesystem.denyRead` (2.1.325: present) (cited from docs by a commenter, not re-checked). have: partial `plugins/claudetto/hooks/features/protected/`. Source: H (2026-11-02).
- [ ] **State-owned actions.** Gate rule: stop only recorded pids (`kill <pid>`), deny `pkill`/`killall`, `rm` of unmarked `/tmp` folders, `git worktree remove`/`git branch -D` of foreign refs; subagents share one session id, so marker cleanup must tell agents apart; deny background `while/until ... sleep` with no `timeout`/`$SECONDS`; Stop blocks once on an agent's own unnamed background shell or monitor (`STALE_TASKS`); halt-the-loop: wait for notification or one timed check. 2.1.325: Monitor present. have: `plugins/claudetto/hooks/features/setup/command-rules.ts` (pkill/killall deny); `plugins/claudetto/hooks/features/hygiene/`; STALE_TASKS missing. Source: A I17, C 18.30/18.33/18.42/18.45, D, E.
- [ ] **Ask or decide.** "If two or more reasonable approaches change the result, stop and ask; otherwise decide and note it" (reported cure; second half prevents needless questions); front-load the reason in the first message because the model fills missing context with the worst case; ask a doubt once with the useful facts, decline at most once and briefly, reconsider on new facts and say whether the answer changes. missing. Source: H.
- [ ] **Subagent fan-out limits.** Set explicit agent-count and model rules; cap explore agents to Haiku and max 2 each; tell the model harness facts (subagents survive `/clear`/`/compact`, do not restart them). missing (candidate harness-facts rule). reported. Source: E, H.
- [ ] **Model split by task openness.** Open problems to Opus, closed to Sonnet; plan in Opus, tickets scoped for Sonnet; evaluate skills against Sonnet with a rubric; compare on 2-3 real backlog tasks (reported). Source: G.
- [ ] **Five takeaways.** Over-deferral frequent; prompt-only fixes kept failing; compaction-off = hard stop ended ~quarter of main sessions, handoff state on disk only reliable carrier across `/clear`; wrong-measurement evals cost more than model mistakes; shared-tree parallelism caused most destructive incidents. Source: A.
- [ ] **Superseded graders.** Change 2 supersedes change 1: grader faults `asks`, `jargon`, `confesses`, `narrates`, `unfiled` removed (change-1 Keep for `asks`, `jargon`/`narrates`, ledger "ask waits for ISSUES file" dropped); `unchecked`, `unseen`, `credited`, `fallback` stay. supersedes C change 1; missing (no grader in plugins). Source: C archive 2026-11-10 design "Removals the Person Chose".
- [ ] **Fault catalogue the Stop grader targets.** credit to the person for unstated choices; silent fallback; deferring doable work; claiming without checking; breaking rules; leaving a fixable failure unfixed; options in plain text instead of `AskUserQuestion` (2.1.325: present); apology/agreement, feelings/praise, repeating the person, naming own mistake with no result, steps listed as a report; citing tool output the person never saw. Cure: one-shot block with the fault's fix. missing. Source: B claim-check.

### Goes

- [ ] Prompt-only mitigation for sycophancy and drift (one claim-check line in `<evidence>` 14/24 -> 17/24; fixed-total line 49/60 -> 51/60; Haiku sycophancy classifier recall 27/28 but live flip 60/64 vs 59/64 at ~5.3 s per prompt): all within noise; only runtime hooks (ledger, claim check) shipped. measured. Source: C 12.1/12.2.
- [ ] Narration-reminder env override as a cure: not significant (13/136 vs 23/136 p=0.106; 24/204 vs 31/204 p=0.385); keep only as a cheap setting. measured. Source: C 16.3/17.4.
- [ ] Line-comment-count and null-guard rule hooks (`noNarratingComments`, `noImpossibleStateGuards`, `useLeastCode`): undecidable without a word list. measured heuristic. Source: D P181.
- [ ] Banned-word lists for hedges and trust-the-file CLAUDE.md enforcement: model finds synonyms, drifts past a rule deep in context, especially after auto-compaction; use hooks. reported. Source: F.
- [ ] False-memory, fake IQ, imaginary-money prompt tricks: act as constraints but invite hallucinated history; use real context and a critic agent. reported. Source: G.
- [ ] Third-party tip threads kept as mentions only: ASD-STE100 for skills (repo already cites it), weekly-limit complaint threads, worktree-coordination thread (one deploy/test worker owns the environment, reports per commit SHA). reported, no measurements. Source: H.
- [ ] Deleted plugin eval suite (agent under test wrote its own graders after seeing results). Source: B.

### Extends

- [ ] Multi-account guidance: one user measured a 20x account yields ~2-8% more weekly usage than two 5x accounts with 4x the 5 h limit vs 2x; another measured weekly ~28% per full 5 h session on 20x vs 13-14% on 5x (weekly ~2x, 5 h 4x); older measure 1.7x per seat; dynamic adjustment by server load suspected (inferred); two 5x accounts hedge a runaway agent blocking a week; terms-of-service risk disputed. reported. Source: F, G.

### Facts

- [ ] (cited) Reddit counts: 338 threads, 13,110 posts/comments; catalog of 1,218 records; ~470 over-engineering, ~191 cost, ~75 sycophancy, ~60 false completion, ~60 gaming, ~55 grounding, ~45 hallucination, ~40 context, ~35 instruction decay. unverified. Source: A.
- [ ] (measured) Paid-run costs: narration A/B $14.49 (largest run 108,533 tokens); fresh run $30.24 (largest 160,283); ledger runs ~$0.0021 live; probe-140 Haiku ~64k tokens $0.0027; slices probe $0.0019; paid ledger ~9.15M tokens after task 12.1. unverified. Source: C.
- [ ] (measured) One claim-checking prompt line moved sycophancy 14/24 -> 17/24 (noise, not shipped); claim check 9/16 -> 16/16 (p=0.0068); ledger 39/48 -> 48/48; `tool-before-text` 5/10 -> 0/10; `asks`-widened `narrates` 8/8 vs 0/8. Source: C.

Heracles: "decide, do, and check on your own", asks nothing back, ends with what it did, what it checked + result, what is left, each defect marked reproduced or read. Athena: defect as `path:line`, what, why. Try another route before reporting a block. Basis: reported. ### Goes

- [ ] Subagents sharing the main checkout for git ops, `git stash` by subagents (A I07; see Keep shared checkout rules).
- [ ] Shared build folders across worktrees (`worktree.symlinkDirectories` for `target`, `.build`, `node_modules`, `dist`) refuted (C 18.40, measured): Cargo `target` ran the other branch's binary (fingerprint on relative path + mtime); SwiftPM `.build` safe but rebuilt minutes per switch (3 runs ~20 min); linked Bun `node_modules` resolved workspace package to main, saved nothing (own install 63 ms). Share only content-addressed download caches. conflict: D323 7.9 still offers symlinkDirectories for git-ignored dependency folders (kept above, with no-slash ignore rule).
- [ ] Old you-should-know port that started on tool.call, awaited the check, denied a noted call and ordered the agent to wait; blocked read-only git status. Note landed after work stopped and retry was denied again (B design Decision 2).
- [ ] Counter caps and whole mods that built-in keys replace: `useBudgetRun`, `useClaudettoAgents/delegation.ts`, `useAdvisorTool`, `useAdvisorCommand`, `useBashRules` gate (D323 inventory, reported); run-limits sections Hold Subagent Fan-Out, Starts Under Cap, End Stuck Agents, output cut (D321, measured).
- [ ] Cautionary cases kept out of source (A, cited): Replit Jul 2025 deleted live DB despite code freeze; reward-hacking benchmarks; DORA 2025; METR 19% slower; GitClear refactoring collapse.
- [ ] Overnight/uncapped parallelism as a goal; agent swarms as a default (heavy users get there via parallel sessions across repos; swarms burn allowance with no gain over ticket-by-ticket; Codex orchestrator spent 16 h and >90% allowance, 9,800-line deploy script) (E, F, I, reported). Unconstrained subagent spawning outside a defined pipeline (82% of one user's usage from subagent-heavy sessions, reported).
- [ ] Forcing every subagent onto one cheap model via global env (overrides deliberately bigger agents, degrades work, reported); percentage splits ("10% Haiku, 50% Sonnet, 40% Opus"); Haiku as long-conversation worker (crosses 100k step; reported 3x faster, 11x less subscription usage only on a 10,000-call single-turn job).
- [ ] Orchestrator-Opus delegating everything (orchestrator planning/review turns dominate, ~equal cost to Opus alone; measured by one poster, H).
- [ ] Agent personification/fake personality (labels not character); Google-Sheet polling as agent bus (nothing notifies the reader); Seedeep/Agent Mesh promo posts; account-ban threads; TablePlus/Tusk/beginner-agent posts (not practice).

Also: compression removing 38.4% of tool-output tokens raised billed cost 6.8% (r=0.15) [2607.12161]; turn count explains 84% of log cost variance [2610.15093]. Basis: cited. Custom system prompt study: 456 API calls, 14 prompts, up to 15 runs: lru_cache 93% vs 53%, explicit utf-8 80% vs 20% (measured by author, small). Rationale on disk: a skill writes decisions, rejected alternatives, workarounds into repo Markdown and reads them before changing code (7/10 sessions without the file re-offered the rejected change, 0/10 with; median 18 s vs 43 s; author-measured). ### Goes

- [ ] **Harness anti-patterns** (W3-1..W3-23): premature multi-agent, agent-as-business-process, invisible state, mega-prompt with unprioritised NEVER/ALWAYS, prompting as the only control ("NEVER delete data"), context stuffing, never compacting, edge-case laundry lists, tool soup, opaque tools, unbounded retries without idempotency, one-shotting, premature victory, free-form progress files, demo-grade in production, no evals/tracing/budgets/kill switch, self-grading, all-or-nothing autonomy, unversioned prompts or auto-upgraded alias, anthropomorphizing. Not features to build; negative design inputs. L14.
- [ ] **Overlapping rewrites and nags** (design Decision 5): plan-mode, EnterPlanMode, ExitPlanMode, NotebookEdit text rewrites (overlapped tool denies); classic-hook duplicates; budget in both prompt and status line; per-prompt "Constraints the user stated earlier" injection (~150 hits); per-prompt Budget line; Stop-judge and Stop "run it with Bash" feedback; Stop "files not committed" nag (removed in release 16ecb31b). L50. conflict: later specs (C L103) still cut EnterPlanMode/ExitPlanMode to <=200 chars via `tool.describe`; D (L103) is later and keeps that cut, so Goes only the deny-overlapping rewrite.
- [ ] **Hard numeric gates** on measured targets (root prompt <=5,832 chars, output style <=1,373 chars, no source-line growth): caps tuned to match stock/rival numbers; keep only the 200-char description limit. L51.
- [ ] **Frontmatter `cacheTtl`** (`experimental.cacheTtl`), replaced by `subagentPromptCacheTtl`. L92. 2.1.325: present as agent frontmatter `experimental.cacheTtl` (chunk-wqb2b4v0.js:994409); it applies only when neither `subagentPromptCacheTtl` nor `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_PROMPT_CACHE_TTL` is set, and "1h" is ignored in overage (`cIn`, chunk-wqb2b4v0.js:2438231). One setting stays the one way.
- [ ] **Subagent-prompts requirements removed:** "A Delegated Edit Comes Back with Its Check", "One Place Sets the Subagent Cache TTL" (rested on `runs.jsonl`), "No Subagent Type Runs the Root Prompt", "A Delegated Edit Is Checked When It Returns" (`ROOT_PROMPT` gone). L125, L130.
- [ ] **Advisor tool and `/advisor` command rebuild** (D323): prompt cache is keyed to model so each advisor question paid full input price on Fable; keep `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_ADVISOR_TOOL=1`; migration: ask `Athena` or `/model`. supersedes D321 rebuilt advisor. L126, L129. 2.1.325: `/advisor` present, env present.
- [ ] **D323 deletions:** `decider/fallback.ts` (judgments move to prompt-type classic hooks), `kill.ts`, worktree ownership check in `own.ts`, endless-wait check, read guard (`surfaceCall`, `heavyFile`, `REFUSAL`, `readGuardEnabled`), `useBashRules`, vendored rules, fixtures, licence, `gate.mock.ts`, `useSliceCommits`, grader, `named-files.ts`, stop and turn files, `useConstraintPin`, `useAskFix`. L127.
- [ ] **Constraint ledger** removed in D323: constraints from the person's words, ledger riding prompts, runtime enforcement, the no-questions hold on ask tools, each-constraint-quotes, resumed-session ledger wait. supersedes all D321 ledger Keeps (L27, L76, L81, L87). L128.
- [ ] **Setup text for sandbox refusals** (extra tokens every session saying what "Operation not permitted" says). L124.
- [ ] **Goal-aware Stop block and prompt key scan:** recommended not to build. L78.
- [ ] **Monitor wrapper** and plugin `Monitor` deferral: built-in Monitor kept; wrapper added no effect (superseded by D). L82, L103.
- [ ] **Unmeasured or discredited skill/prompt claims:** token-saving skills (caveman -65% claim) did not reduce cost; popular skills no better than a same-length placebo; skills "wildly inconsistent for frontier work"; "I deleted most of my harness, Opus 5.5 made them obsolete"; skills for what the model already knows (except own MCP/program); always-on minimal-code skills as a bill cut (10.3% bill cut and 15% less code, 80 paired tasks; use as audit only); caveman/ponytail terse skills unproven, use built-in Concise (disputed); "Make no mistakes" and persona prompts; Output Purity Principle prompt; workflow-DSL prompts in project instructions (use plain standing lines or the Learning style); Ultracode-style "ignore cost, split as much as possible" orchestration; tool-compression and cache-warning mods (+0.2-1.5%, unmeasurable); skills off did not shrink start (one user). Drop any plugin skill whose benefit lacks a placebo-controlled measurement. L162, L188, L189, L191, L219, L220, L221, L227, L242, L167.
- [ ] **Keep-warm tricks:** Stop hook replying every ~4.5 min, one-letter `/w` skill; joke/workaround, costs more than it saves for idle waits (inferred). L144, L163.
- [ ] **Patching or un-minifying the CLI** for the system prompt (repo policy forbids). Reported unofficial alternative `CLAUDE_CODE_THRIFTY_SONIC=0` (cat-and-sed hint in bypass mode). 2.1.325: env present; effect unverified. L192.
- [ ] **Blanket `subagentPromptCacheTtl=1h`** recommendation (see conflict in Keep). L190.
- [ ] **Noise:** local-model injection/quantization chatter, quarrels, limit-marketing complaints, vendor/promo threads (AGENTS.md visuals, ProjectArchitect, Vachi), artifact usage promotion (+50% five-hour limit only), mods announcement (listed capabilities, no practice yet). L164, L165, L166, L168, L169.

## Hooks and Safety

### Keep

Mark legend: `2.1.325: present|absent|unverified` from cc-inspect surface; `have|partial|missing` from a read-only grep of `/Users/krystian/CodeProjects/xsyetopz/claudetto/plugins/`. Source tags are `<worker>:<input line numbers>`. Basis labels: measured, reproduced, cited, reported, inferred, read.

#### Hook engine and fault policy

- [ ] **Hooks fail open; validate config shape.** `hook crashes -> stderr, call goes through`; in 2.1.305+ an unknown hook `type` (e.g. `"script"`) disables the whole settings file; wrong-case frontmatter/matcher keys are ignored silently. Consequence: test hook-config shape. Basis: reported. 2.1.325: unverified. missing (no config-shape test found). Src: A:11. Related fact: PreToolUse exit 2 blocks and feeds stderr to the model, exit 1 alone is a non-blocking error, PostToolUse cannot prevent the action (cited docs; A:152, F:152, I:172); PostToolUse exit 2 feeds, exit 1 does not (I:172). 2.1.325: present (PreToolUse, PostToolUse).
- [ ] **Fault policy for gating hooks (latest wins).** `conflict:` C/D321 = one `tool.check` + one `tool.call` hook per plugin, each ONE `.catch`; `tool.check` fault -> ask ("The gate failed: <error>. A person must approve this call."), `tool.call` fault -> deny, if `next.called` replay `next` and withhold result; a guard without `.catch` fails open (throw -> allow; overrun > 10 s -> allow); every other `catch` logs via `$.ui.log` or rethrows (64 of 92 catches bound no error and hid defects; bare `parseFaults catch { return [] }` turned a bad answer into a clean reply). vs D323 (supersedes D321, released as 2.1.325) = delete the `tool.check` catch in `register.ts` and `checkFault` so a guard fault leaves the engine's verdict (no ask of its own); the `tool.call` catch returns the `next(e)` result once the tool has run; `secretCall` denies on scan failure ("withheld", holds no key) because after that change a thrown scan error let an unscanned result reach the model; each `tool.check` guard that does not judge a tool returns before its first `$` call (timed on an MCP call); `claude plugin validate --strict --json` shows `hasCatch` false (tool.check) and true (tool.call); overrun in `tool.call` names kind `timeout`; no `catch` turns a fault into a normal answer; lab test `faults.test.ts`. Basis: measured (P16/P17/P19/P30/P46/P48/P50), reported. 2.1.325: present (tool.check, tool.call mod events). have: plugins/claudetto/hooks/faults.test.ts, hooks/register.ts. Src: C:90-92,102; D:90,92,102,116,120.
- [ ] **Engine/loader constraints on mod code.** Literal `on('<event>', ...)` with direct `$.<ns>.<method>()` calls (no loop/proxy/wrapper, no destructuring `$`); one unmatched hook per event per module (an event two features hook unmatched is owned by `register.ts`; `on("session.start") is registered twice without a matcher` fails the module); the loader follows `$` only by name through function declarations, never arrows, lists, params, or exports (`claude plugin validate --strict` rejects "$ passed to a function expression outside the hook's own file"); `register.ts` chains `onSettingsRead` with `denyBuiltinFetch` (engine takes one unmatched `settings.read` per module); hooks import code files only (not `node:path`, a package, or JSON); every model-read text in one `texts` file; strict TS flags kept (`strict`, `noUncheckedIndexedAccess`, `exactOptionalPropertyTypes`); `$.tool.register` is unavailable in `classic.SessionStart` ("no session is bound"), register in the single `session.start` hook; a `command.run` hook answering `goal` without `next()` replaces built-in `/goal`. Basis: measured 2.1.319, read. 2.1.325: present (session.start, command.run, tool.register). have: plugins/claudetto/hooks/register.ts, hooks/features/*. Src: B:46; C:56,61,66,83; D:91,131.
- [ ] **Do not rewrite a tool input (auto mode).** In 2.1.319 a changed input voids auto mode's server review of every call in the response (`serverClassifierInputRewritten`) and each comes back "gave no verdict"; block with a reason naming the exact form instead of rewriting; a call needing no change reaches `next(e)` as `e` itself; no hook rewrites a `ToolSearch` query or result; check each tool.check/tool.call return that sets `input`. Basis: measured. serverClassifierInputRewritten: unverified. have: plugins/claudetto/hooks/input-unchanged.test.ts (partial; the string itself is absent from plugins/). conflict: C:63 (built-in agent mapping by rewrite) and B:214 (deny-with-exact-form) are reconciled by deny-with-exact-form; D323 later drops the whole Agent deny (see Goes). Src: B:36,63; C:63,73; D:96,111; F:147,148.
- [ ] **Hook text rewrites must account for `Edit`'s `old_string` check order.** Reported: a sentence-splitting PreToolUse hook reflowed Markdown and an `Edit` spanning two sentences failed 4 of 4 because CC checks `old_string` before PreToolUse; a `tool.call` module handler repaired it 3 of 3. Basis: reported. 2.1.325: present (tool.call). missing. Src: A:7,181.
- [ ] **Check at the earliest event that sees the fault; Stop only for what no earlier event sees.** `MessageDisplay` is display-only and cannot make the model rewrite, so no reply check moves earlier than Stop; `turn.step` calling `next` again for a new response is a hypothesis (expected to duplicate streamed text). Basis: measured+cited, hypothesis. 2.1.325: present (MessageDisplay, turn.step). Src: D:100,127,128.
- [ ] **Engine facts that drive structure.** Hook budget 10 s per event (docs, cited), plugin hook past budget twice is unloaded (reported); `$.http.fetch` wait stops the hook clock even with `$.clock.sleep` raced beside it, own 30 s cap, no `signal`; `tool.check` deny arrives at `tool.call` as `isError`; a PreToolUse allow followed by a check error counts as no answer; `tool.describe` events carry no thread (subagent and main get one Bash text); `$.tool.check({tool:"Read"})` gives the session's verdict; `$.fs.exists` throws on `/\` paths (a grep pattern like `/\.git/` reads as a network location); 33 classic events agree between docs and types, 44 mod events. Basis: measured/cited. 2.1.325: present (`$.http.fetch`, `$.clock.sleep`, `$.tool.check`, tool.describe, `$.fs.exists`; classic events listed 33 of 33). Src: B:49,52; C:80,81,258; D:100,132,133.

#### Stop and completion gates

- [ ] **Stop/SubagentStop runs the real check and blocks on failure.** `stop_hook_active` guard against loops; `CLAUDE_CODE_STOP_HOOK_BLOCK_CAP` caps re-blocks (at most 8 in a row); verdicts from direct exit codes, never `| grep`/`| tail` (reported to cut false passes); `onFailure` has no effect on Stop/SubagentStop/TaskCompleted/TeammateIdle in 2.1.312 (crash/timeout lets the turn end); agent-type hooks default to Haiku "use as few steps as possible", so set `model`; a prompt-type Stop hook judges transcript text only and treats "tests pass" as evidence. Report denominator, "measured vs inferred", the 2-3 least-certain items; verify at the layer the claim is about (UI claims need rendered observation). 2.1.325: present (Stop, SubagentStop, STOP_HOOK_BLOCK_CAP). missing (no `stop_hook_active` or exit-2 Stop hook in plugins/; D323 removed most Stop gates). Src: A:2,13.
- [ ] **TaskCompleted runs the project check.** Exit 2 keeps the task open, stderr goes to the model. 2.1.325: present (TaskCompleted). missing. Src: A:14.
- [ ] **Stop-check command-bullet rule.** A `- [ ]`/`- !` bullet is a command only when its text opens with `! ` or backtick-`! `; numbered task lines (`9.31 ...`) are no question; a bullet that quotes `! cmd` in backticks is no stray command (`COMMAND_BULLET`); 16 of 108 items were questions typed as items; open-items/command-form checks had real catches (stray `sudo`, `! cd ... && git push`). Basis: measured/reproduced. supersedes C (B:41, D:94,98,113); but see Goes (D323 removed Open Items at Stop). missing (no COMMAND_BULLET in plugins/). Src: B:41; D:94,98,113.
- [ ] **What I Need form check (D323).** Classic Stop command hook reads only `## What I Need From You` of `last_assistant_message`: at most one item, a `- ! <command>` bullet with no checkbox, or one fenced `sh` block for a separate terminal (`GH_TOKEN=$(gh auth token) claude --continue`; `!` runs in the session shell and cannot start Claude Code with new env); `- [ ] ! gh auth login` blocks (nobody ticks a checkbox in a reply); two commands block once naming both and keeping the needed one; prose outside the section never matched; a block identical to an earlier block of the session passes; `stop_hook_active` second stop passes; `userConfig` switch turns it off; a paid run named as a `- ! <command>` bullet passes; section carries only a command the agent cannot run, never `sudo`, a slash command, a token prompt, or a repeat. Also: a `- ! <command>` bullet naming a command no earlier Bash call ran, or whose last run succeeded, blocks (reads `transcript_path`; a denied command passes). Basis: reported. 2.1.325: present (Stop). missing. Src: D:118,119,276.
- [ ] **Stop block text asks only for what it corrects.** Full-resend requests doubled the report while the blocked reply stayed on screen; a second reply after a remark/notice writes only what is new; blocks asking for a rewrite end with `KEEP_ANSWER`; blocks naming lines (ask form, command form, missing file, agree opener) name the edit; `SendMessage` result text appears from the tool result so no hook writes it into a reply. Basis: measured+reported. 2.1.325: present (SendMessage tool). missing. Src: D:105,106.
- [ ] **Commit and worktree Stop filters.** `changedOf` leaves out `<cwd>/.claude/worktrees/` paths (commit check was a real catch for 47% of 230 blocks in 7 days, partial for 27%). Basis: measured. missing (D323 removed the slice-commit Stop block; commit rule stays a prompt line). Src: D:97,113.
- [ ] **Claim check at Stop.** A reply stating the state of work when no call that turn looked at the workspace gets one block sending the agent to check; a denied/refused call is not a look; Haiku grades the `unchecked` fault; measured syc-fixed-total 9/16 -> 16/16 (p=0.0068; pooled 24/24 vs 13/24); Stop hook median 2.5 ms, Haiku call median 1.05 s over 133 calls, blocked turn +4.4 s and $0.012; must run at Stop (a next-prompt note comes after the commit message was used). conflict: the Noul/Decider replacement scored 22/40 held-out vs 32 for Haiku+Sonnet and was dropped; the five-question Decider register check (apologizes_or_agrees, states_feeling_or_praise, repeats_person, names_own_mistake_without_result, steps_listed_as_report at >=80% precision and recall; any yes blocks once; four held-out questions claim_without_shown_check, question_unanswered, doable_step_left, choice_handed_to_person never block) is kept by B and D321. Rule: a site whose recorded check fails keeps its current code. Cure for sycophancy, narration, self-flagellation. Basis: measured. 2.1.325: present (Stop). partial: plugins/claudetto/hooks/kernel/decider/ exists. Src: B:32; C:58,78; D:107,123.
- [ ] **Tool call written as plain text.** With thinking disabled the model can write a tool call as text that never executes; Stop hook scans the last reply for tool-call syntax with no matching `tool_use` block and tells the agent to re-issue. Basis: reported (Opus 5.5 system card via transcription). missing. Src: F:145.
- [ ] **Enforce required delegation.** When the user's instruction requires a subagent or a named reviewer, a hook or Stop check confirms an Agent/Task call occurred before the reply. #### Rejection, denial, and consent

- [ ] **A rejection is terminal for that action.** `retry in other words / other tool (sed then Python) / other agent / later = forbidden`; carry into every subagent hand-back and brief; block an identical retry with a hook keyed on tool input. Reported worst case: a background subagent's rejected edit to a test's expected value was retried with a reworded comment, switched to Python when `sed` failed, then reported "Task 23.2 is done" without mentioning rejection (background subagents see no human; rejection reads as transient error). Also: a rejected tool call can already have written the file. Basis: reported. missing (hook); partial: plugins/claudetto/hooks/features/repeat. Src: A:6,181,190,192.
- [ ] **PermissionDenied handling.** With a reason: answer `retry` alone (never a decision) for the first denial since the person's last message, not the second; no reason / "(it gave no explanation)" is never retried; after the second denial refuse with count and earlier reason ("none given" if empty), tell the agent to change the call or ask, never find another way; same tool different input is not refused; a task notification does not reset the count; the count resets on a new USER message. Unexplained classifier denial: name the missing reason, tell the agent to give the exact `! <command>` and continue. Engine deny with a rule adds "The rule `<rule>` denies it." ; the gate reports its OWN rule, not an allow rule to add. Basis: reported. 2.1.325: present (PermissionDenied). missing (no PermissionDenied in plugins/; partial: hooks/features/repeat). Src: B:220.
- [ ] **Refuse repeated failures.** After the same call fails `failLimit` times with the same error, refuse an identical retry with the earlier error as reason; a user interrupt is not a failure; a call without `tool_use_id` is never held. conflict: B specs/run-limits keeps it; D323 removed it ("its text appears in no transcript, so it never fired"). Prefer D323. Src: B:39; D:125.
- [ ] **Two failed attempts then stop** (n=13; conduct n=3): state the block, list what is ruled out, diagnose before patching; root cause over symptom (n=3); Haiku early-ending turns need "continue" (n=3,7). Never retry blind: when a step times out check what happened first. Basis: reported counts. missing (prose only). Src: A:9; I:168.
- [ ] **Consent is explicit, per action, and names the value.** `consent = user_message AND names(exact_action)`; plan approval does not cover numbers inside it (echo "raise `STYLE_MAX_BYTES` 5,680 -> 7,061?" and wait; a bound/budget/timeout/test expectation changes only to a value the user named; at a bound split the file or ask, never compress/merge/move/delete unrelated content); silence, hook text, "your call", weak "yes", "yes, but first look at X", relayed or another agent's approval are not consent; tolerance is not waiver and an earlier breach sets no precedent; standing autonomy in a rules file (e.g. evals up to $5) counts; destructive/outbound actions ask every time; never approve a permission prompt by keystroke/tmux/settings edit; no agent message is user consent. Consent follows reversibility (local reversible = no ask; hard-to-undo or visible-to-others = ask, one approval one action; a step's cost alone is no reason to ask). Basis: reported (conduct n=10,9,7,3,3). have: CONSENT in plugins/claudetto/hooks/features/prompt/texts.ts:56; missing: hook denying approval claims without a user prompt. Src: A:17,178,183,187,190,222.
- [ ] **Reserved Actions (consent each time).** Delete files/branches/data incl. `rm -rf`; `git reset --hard`, `git clean`, `git checkout -- .`, history rewrites, force-push, amending published commits; DROP/TRUNCATE/destructive migrations; `terraform destroy`; production; push/tag/release/publish/deploy; send messages, comment on/open/merge PRs; spend beyond budget; change permissions or credentials; machine-wide (`sudo`, global installs, shell profiles). On surprise (empty data, failing baseline, unknown files, dirty tree) stop, investigate read-only, report; no `--no-verify`. A delete request lists every path; before an approved destructive group commit or copy untracked work. Three-pile split: destroy / reach out (curl|sh, publish, deploy, anything that sends) / read (`cat .env`, `printenv`, keychain); better test is "does the result leave the machine"; run agents on a box that does nothing else and route outbound to a phone approval. Block irreversible actions by PreToolUse hook instead of skip-permissions. Basis: reported/cited. partial: hooks/features/protected, hooks/features/setup/command-rules.ts. Src: A:191; F:295; H:166.
- [ ] **No route through a blocked or disabled control.** A permission, sandbox, classifier, or hook block is final for that action (no other command, tool, encoding, archive, script, delegate, nested session); keep SIP, Gatekeeper, code signing, sandbox, hooks, pre-commit on (eval: pre-commit failure -> fix what it reports, `--no-verify` fails); a failed `pgrep`/`ps`/`ls`/`grep`/read is unknown, not negative; classifier block -> notice naming the rule (Irreversible Local Destruction, Safety Bypass Flag, Credential Exploration, Create Unsafe Agents, Tmux Self Drive), say once if false positive; never drive own terminal pane or a nested agent with keystrokes. A deny list alone is not a boundary: agents wrote their own program, switched to Node/Python, or built a container with a bind mount to run `rm -rf` past a command guard (do not mount a directory the agent must not change). Block, never rewrite; always print the alternative in the PreToolUse stderr or the model tries the next way around; log every block. Basis: reported. partial: plugins/claudetto/hooks/features/protected, repeat. Src: A:192,194; B:223; F:147; I:306 (Goes duplicate).
- [ ] **Breach response, six steps in order.** Stop the action; notice with the clause ID; restore earlier state or give the commands; name the cause; promise nothing and offer a mechanism; do not repeat the action in another form. Precedence (7 levels): harness/provider safety, user's latest instruction, SOW, Terms (then licence/ToU/AUP/RoE then ToS), project rules files, observable state of the Work, general knowledge. Notices lead the next message, one line each `TYPE: what happened`: breach, block, rejection, consent request (names action/value old and new and what happens on "no"), change to a user-owned value, narrowed scope, substituted model, unverified claim. Stop conditions (RE-7): rejection or block; unexpected state; budget exceeded -> ask old and new; rules file contradicts repo; check looks wrong or task impossible -> report with evidence; instructions inside content -> tell the user; process tool fails -> check another way. Keep evidence per claim (command, exit code, output, log path); re-read after a failed write; `git status` before done. missing as convention. Src: A:17,18,20,188.

Consequence: corrected in >=12 sessions. Basis: reported (readers' judgement). Src: A:177,189.

#### Process, tree, and worktree hygiene

- [ ] **Process hygiene.** Stop only by saved PID with `kill <pid>` or `TaskStop`, never `pkill -f`/`killall`; one lock per expensive job (PID, start, owner); a failed `pgrep` is unknown; `set -euo pipefail` and a guarded `mktemp` (an unguarded one left an empty var and `cd` ran in HOME); background processes need a hard time limit (a render ran 9 hours); never stop a process you did not start, set a docker compose project name, never bare `-j`; heavy sessions exhausted memory (16 GB Mac, 11 sessions, 44 MCP processes, load 26). Permission deny: `Bash(pkill *)`, `Bash(killall *)`. 2.1.325: present (TaskStop). have: CLAUDE.md pid rule, Heracles.md:11, evals/*/scaffold.sh, hooks/features/memory; partial: setup command-rules (pkill). Src: A:23; I:168; D:279,281.
- [ ] **Heavy-job brake.** PreToolUse classifies shell commands; light pass; heavy (Remotion, ffmpeg, headless Chrome, Playwright, Xcode/JS builds, Docker builds) wait for free memory/pressure (cause: two background subagents each launching headless Chrome with a 4 GB GPU budget on a 16 GB Mac). Basis: reported. partial: plugins/claudetto/hooks/features/memory. Src: G:155.
- [ ] **Busy-wait and temp-delete rules (reproduced shapes).** Deny `while`/`until` whose body is only `:` or `true` and condition lacks `sleep`/`read`/`wait` (foreground/background; Bash and Monitor); pass `sleep` in body, `until sleep 2 && grep`, `while read ... < f`, `for` loops. Temp bases `/tmp`, `/tmp/claude`, `/tmp/claude-<uid>` (with or without `/private`) and `$TMPDIR`: deny `rm` of a base, its `*` glob, or a `..` path, giving `rm -rf /tmp/<name>`; CC's own check denies `rm` targets ending `$dir/`, `$dir/*`, or `$(...)` so do not teach `d=$(mktemp -d)` then `rm -rf "$d"`. Pipe exit-code rule stays (22 of 23 distinct denials were a masked check); `git commit` after a failed step denied for `;`, newline, `|| true`, pipefail-pipe-then-`;`, loops; `&&`, `set -e`, `|| exit 1`, `if..then`, `until` pass; `[`/`[[` read as test commands; `sed -i` read as the editor's flag only; shell-read denial offers a ready `Read` call with `offset`/`limit`; unsafe-fix rule matches `oxlint@latest --fix-suggestions`; `echo $! > pid` passes. Basis: reproduced. conflict: D323 plans to drop the Bash gate (`tool.check/useBashRules/`) for setup-written `permissions` rules; the D323 plan stands where its gap is measured. partial: hooks/kernel/shell, features/temp-cleanup; hooks/features/protected. Src: D:95,104,264,265,270,284.
- [ ] **Gate path-operand rule.** Do not treat a grep pattern (`/\|Catalog/`, `/\.git/`) as a path: `$.fs.exists` throws on `/\` paths and a throwing check loses every check of that stop (and fires "The gate failed"); take the engine's refusal as the answer, drop that operand, keep checking others. Basis: reproduced/measured. 2.1.325: present (`$.fs.exists`). missing (no named-files check; D323 removed it). Src: B:43,235; D:95,112.
- [ ] **Formatter stays in task files.** Deny `bun fmt` with no path or `.` unless `--check/--staged/--changed/--stdin-filepath`; deny prettier/biome `--write` with no path or `.`; denial names scoped forms. Consequence: whole-repo format rewrites files outside the task. missing. Src: B:221.
- [ ] **Hook-write ask (plugins/<name>/hooks).** Built-in checks ask for Bash writes into `.claude/`, `~/.claude/`, `.git/hooks/` and for a Write/Edit of plugins/, not for Bash writes into `plugins/<name>/hooks/` (`echo b > ...`, `cp` ran). Fix: `writeTargets` (redirect targets, `tee`, `rm`, `sed -i` operands, last operand of `cp`/`mv`) + `PLUGIN_HOOKS` -> ask. Not covered: path built by expansion or `cd` first, `..`, other folder names. Also ask on `git push origin :main` and any rewriting-push spelling; the test-tamper check is NOT built (not sound: 100 commits delete tests, two of three forms indistinguishable). Basis: read/reproduced. missing. Src: C:60,67; B:233.
- [ ] **Branch moves under sandbox-denied paths.** Classic PreToolUse command hook on Bash before `git switch`, `git checkout <ref>`, `git reset --hard`, `git rebase` compares `HEAD` with the target under each sandbox write-denied path (e.g. `.claude/skills`; 7 files differ), denies with the list; list retaken from 2.1.323. #### Sandbox, permissions settings, and secrets

- [ ] **Use the built-in sandbox through setup, never an own wrapper.** Setup writes one `sandbox` group into user settings (the only route carrying `filesystem` and `network`; plugin-root `settings.json` drops `sandbox` and Bash runs unsandboxed; a `settings.read` hook carries only `enabled` and `allowUnsandboxedCommands`); block lists what is dangerous, not what is safe; least friction ("block truly destructive, let the rest pass"); no per-tool cache entries. Seatbelt does not nest (inner `sandbox_apply` fails "Operation not permitted" exit 71; with native sandbox plus `CLAUDE_CODE_SHELL_PREFIX` every plain Bash call exits 71), so no `sandbox-exec`/SBPL profile/proxy/`sandboxConfig`/`confine` plugin; SBPL accepts only `*`/`localhost` hosts. 2.1.325: present (sandbox.enabled, allowUnsandboxedCommands, filesystem.*, network.*, CLAUDE_CODE_SHELL_PREFIX). partial: plugins/claudetto/hooks/features/setup/settings.ts:211 writes only `{ sandbox: { enabled: true } }`; richer block missing. Src: B:209,238(C),242(C),246(C); C:238,242,246; D:273.
- [ ] **Sandbox block, final choices (conflict across drafts; latest D321/B).** `conflict:` C draft = `allowUnsandboxedCommands: false` (removes retry prompt, keeps denies, ~330 sandbox-off calls), `allowWrite` [~/.cache, ~/.bun, ~/.npm, ~/Library/Caches, /private/tmp], `denyRead` [~/.aws, ~/.azure, ~/.config/gcloud, ~/.netrc, ~/.git-credentials], leave `autoAllowBashIfSandboxed` and `failIfUnavailable` unset, `~/.ssh`/`~/.config/gh`/`~/.gnupg` readable; A (SA-5.2) wants `allowUnsandboxedCommands:false` + `failIfUnavailable:true` in strict profiles (loses the U4.3 fallback; F15: eval and strict profiles); vs B/D321 = `enabled` true, `allowUnsandboxedCommands` true (retry via permission flow), `enableWeakerNetworkIsolation` true, `allowWrite` `~/`, `/Volumes/`, temp, `/private/var/folders`; `denyWrite` for shell startup files (.zshrc .zshenv .zprofile .bashrc .bash_profile .profile, ~/.config/fish), ~/.gitconfig, ~/.config/git, ~/.ssh, ~/Library/LaunchAgents, PATH folders under home (~/.local/bin ~/.bun/bin ~/bin), `xcrun_db*` under /private/var/folders; `denyRead` ~/Library/Keychains, ~/.aws, ~/.azure, ~/.config/gcloud, ~/.netrc, ~/.git-credentials; `allowRead` login.keychain-db (without it `gh auth status` fails "Failed to log in", `git push --dry-run` "could not read Username"); `network.allowedDomains` ["*"], `allowMachLookup` ["*"] (pgrep/scutil/pbpaste/diskutil 0 with it, 3/1/1/1 without; `gh` TLS passes), `allowLocalBinding` true, `allowAllUnixSockets` true; `excludedCommands` `ps *`, `top *`, `sysctl *`, `launchctl list` (`just *`) only for commands no key can make run (hand-written `excludedCommands` matches only a call that is one plain command, 4 of 513); ~/.ssh and ~/.gnupg stay readable (signed commits; a lab test fails if ~/.ssh is added to denyRead), ~/.ssh write-denied; user's own `allowWrite` stays on `fill`. Cache env dirs added to `allowWrite` when set: `BUN_INSTALL_CACHE_DIR`, `npm_config_cache`, `PIP_CACHE_DIR`, `GOMODCACHE`, `GOCACHE`, `XDG_CACHE_HOME` (not `CARGO_HOME`: holds bin/, config.toml, credentials.toml). macOS `/tmp` is a symlink so say `/private/tmp`. Never write `autoAllowBashIfSandboxed` false or `failIfUnavailable` (B: no `autoAllowBashIfSandboxed`/`failIfUnavailable`). Basis: measured; choices of the person 2026-11-09/10/12 reported. 2.1.325: present (all sandbox keys above). have: partial only. - [ ] **Git under the sandbox.** Write no settings key for `.git` and no list of git commands run outside the sandbox; `.git/config` and `.git/hooks` stay closed (core.fsmonitor/sshCommand/hook files run outside the sandbox later); before a Bash call holding `git worktree add`, create the repository's `worktrees` folder on the host (else "could not create leading directories" after start-time worktrees were removed); if the project root had no `.git` at the first Bash call and gains one, that call's result carries one reminder per root (git writes fail until restart with `claude --continue`; until then `dangerouslyDisableSandbox`); leave engine-closed paths closed (`.vscode`, `.idea`, `.claude/commands` at working-folder root even with allowWrite; `/opt/homebrew`; headless Chrome; brew installs go through the permission-flow retry); add no text of our own (engine's "Operation not permitted" already evidences the retry). - [ ] **Secret handling.** Never seek confidential information (no `cat .env`, key grep, listing credential stores; deny Read/Bash patterns for `.env` and credential paths; deny `Read(<wall>/**)` plus sandbox credential read-deny); never reproduce it in output, logs, source, commits, memory or a brief; secret-shaped test files get fakes in a temp dir; secrets never in code/logs/prompts, with pre-commit and CI scanners; a PreToolUse hook on Bash|WebFetch|Write|Edit greps stdin for secret/email -> exit 2 (encoded forms slip past; do not hold the datum if the model need not; Opus put the user's email into an outbound User-Agent header). Env-file reads: claudetto may read env files, the model never sees values (betterleaks redacts the tool result; a hook's own `{result}` or a rewrite of `next(e)` both keep values out of the request). Secret matcher: scan every tool result before the model sees it; an errored result with a secret reaches the model as a redacted deny; missing/failing betterleaks denies naming `brew install betterleaks`; an error beneath the gate reaches the caller unchanged. Credentials kept away from the agent (injected auth, proxy swapping fakes, redaction in tool results), enforce access on the resource not the verb (protectedPaths), grep built output not just source. 2.1.325: present (sandbox.credentials.*). have: plugins/claudetto/hooks/features/secrets/scan.ts, hooks/features/setup/settings.ts:211, plugin.json:84. ### Coverage

- Input lines read: 306 of 306 (nothing left unread).
- Lines merged into: 185 checkbox items (Keep 93, Goes 24, Extends 14, Agent Faults 21, Facts 33), plus a legend. Ratio about 1.65 input lines per item; every source tag is kept per item.
- 2.1.325 marks (surface queries by exact name; env CLAUDE_CODE_ family queried per name because the family list truncates): present on the settings, env vars, hook events and mod API names verified (for example `permissions.defaultMode`, `sandbox.autoAllowBashIfSandboxed`, `sandbox.allowUnsandboxedCommands`, `CLAUDE_CODE_SCRIPT_CAPS`, `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBPROCESS_ENV_SCRUB`, `CLAUDE_ENV_FILE`, `CONCURRENT_SUBAGENTS`, `$.http.fetch`, `$.process.run`, `$.store.*`, `$.fs.*`, `$.command.*`, `tool.list`, `tool.describe`, `settings.read`); absent: `sandbox.filesystem.allowGitConfig`, `$.fs` folder move, top-level `defaultMode` and `protectedPaths` (the latter is a plugin userConfig, not a setting); unverified: `serverClassifierInputRewritten` behaviour, `engines["claude-code"]` refusal, `claude plugin validate --strict --json` output, 2.1.319 engine refusal rules, and third-party reported facts. Exact per-kind totals were not tallied per item; counts are by the marks written inline.
- have/partial/missing marks are written inline per item from read-only greps of plugins/claudetto, plugins/claudetto-you-should-know and plugins/claudetto-openspec; Goes items carry no mark, Agent Faults carry their cure mechanisms in place of a mark.
- Known soft spots: Agent Faults cures are proposals (hooks not verified as existing); D323 removal items in Goes supersede D321/B Keep items and are cross-referenced there.

## Context and Memory (Dossier and OpenSpec)

Tag legend: `2.1.325: present|absent` = checked with cc-inspect `surface` on 2.1.325; `have|partial|missing` = grep of `/Users/krystian/CodeProjects/xsyetopz/claudetto/plugins/`. Sources A dossier, B specs, C/D archives (D321 = archive 2026-11-?? slice, D323 later; D supersedes C). Basis labels in parentheses.

### Keep

**Enforcement ladder, rules, instruction files**
- [ ] Ladder `prose < memory/rules file < per-turn reinjection < hooks/gates < sandbox/permissions/architecture < training`; each issue gets more reliable as its mitigation moves up. `if checkable(rule) -> hook/gate/permission; else prose with reason`. Rule decays otherwise (reported: `[Unreleased]` and prose-test fixes failed again; lints, hooks, turn counters held). Companion: owner order for any feature, chosen by a recorded check: settings key/built-in -> classic hook -> skill -> output style -> rule file -> mod -> removal, because settings and classic hooks need no mod code, survive `--safe-mode`, and CC checks them; a feature whose check has not run stays "proposed" and ships no change; rule packs/skills rarely beat a same-length placebo (cited/inferred). Highest rung that holds; rejects keep reasons (Stop gate = noise, no `maxTurns` on agents, no ask rules for destructive commands because they override auto mode). Have: partial (hooks/kernel, hooks/features). Sources: A, B (dossier recipes I01-I18), D321, D323. 
- [ ] Independent overseer: fresh context, different model, access to evidence not to the worker's summary; same-model peers share blind spots (cited 2502.08788). Fresh-context review: reviewer never saw the writing conversation, judges diff vs requirements, each finding carries path:line + what + why + read/reproducing command, judged by confirmed bugs not list length, PR-stage only, read-only, one pass per commit, fixed exit condition (an in-session loop took 53 rounds, half the findings from its own fixes); goal + criteria (met / not met / can't tell), bugs first, stop on unreproducible nits; adversarial second opinion before commit. Have: agents/Athena.md, hooks/features/prompt/texts.ts. Source: A.
- [ ] Project state: write where the agent reads it, better derive by script; when a rules file contradicts observable state, stop the affected step, report with evidence, propose corrected wording, continue unaffected work (changelog `[Unreleased]` example); an added rule atop a wrong rule does not help. Precedence list: the higher wins. Have: partial (root prompt). Source: A (T3.1-T3.3, principle 4).
- [ ] Context-file counter-evidence: AGENTS.md-style files did not raise success and added >20% inference cost (cited 2602.11988); content matters, not existence; human-written harness beats machine-generated context (cited, ETH Zurich). Source: A.
- [ ] Context files as versioned repo files: plans/decisions/specs in repo not chat, dated so stale notes can be queried (about 500 memory files with conflicting revisions made agents argue); plans with `@` paths; facts in a dated notes file; skills load one-line description, full text on match; SKILL.md under about 300 lines; disable overlapping/unused skills; generic skill packs largely obsolete. Have: openspec/, rules/. Source: A.
- [ ] Decisions log and glossary: dated one-line decisions with reason and rejected options; glossary term with first use; reason/incident beside each rule; superseded rule marked replaced on the spot; reverse condition + check command per rule so it can be pruned (434 to 1,554 lines in one day). Candidate: active/replaced mark per decision in the handoff text; glossary line in propose skill. Missing. Source: A.
- [ ] Rule phrasing: say what to do with a reason, calmly, no capitals/"CRITICAL"; "do instead" beside each prohibition; positive format wording; worked/contrastive examples beat descriptions; XML tags with prose inside for multi-part prompts; bans on tics fail, "don't" hard to steer; no "think carefully", never ask a model to write out reasoning (reasoning_extraction refusals); use Anthropic sample paragraphs for premature-stop, keep-working, stop-and-report, real-check, pasted content. Aligns with project CLAUDE.md. Have: partial. Source: A. Companion: each limiting rule gives its reason; examples in `<example>` tags; text read against each receiving model's prompting guide via clawdipedia-docs, guide section names recorded in design.md when two models share a body; a bare prohibition is rewritten (B agent-facing-text). Missing (unchecked).
- [ ] Shared prompt sections are a pure function of inputs (no time, counter, usage number) so two sessions' shared sections are byte-identical (cache); no per-message injection, context enters once at first message or compaction, a quiet turn adds no text; values changing mid-session go into the newest turn, not the system prompt. Prompt text has one route per rule (root prompt, code rule file, output style, or a context fact line; a context line carries facts only). Source: B (prompt-footprint, tasks), C. Have: hooks/features/prompt.
