Process: conduct, scope, evidence, planning, files
- [ ] **Truly stuck means only the user can act.** Stuck only when the next step needs what only the user holds (sudo, password, hardware, interactive login): give the exact `! <command>`, finish every independent part, no `- ! sudo ...`/`- ! /login` bullet; an authorized `.env` token is used by the agent (calling it "a step only you can do" fails); a general request for help fails. Only commands the agent cannot run, once each; heading `## What I Need From You` capitalizes every word; report each experiment and result. Questions go to `AskUserQuestion`, only user-only commands are `- [ ] ! <cmd>` items (not typed questions as `- [ ]`). Sources: B, D323 specs/agent-conduct, C tasks 18.20. have: partial (claudetto texts, Concise rule; no Stop check found).
- [ ] **Text between tool calls holds a finding.** Open with the outcome or deciding fact; between calls put a finding, result, or error ("The hook has no retry; ...gate.ts:234 registers one check." passes, "Now I will read the file" fails). Messages to an agent hold only task, inputs it lacks, done-check; no greeting, thanks, readable context; one message per agent, follow-up only when the report leaves the task undone. Replies to a person: plain English, code in backticks cited `file:line`. Low-risk work starts at once: state the reasonable reading and go on. Source: B specs/agent-conduct. partial (root prompt / Concise.md).
- [ ] **Second reading settled by evidence.** From conversation, repo, history, plans; state the reading taken; ask via AskUserQuestion only for what the user alone holds (intent, preference, meaning of own term); never act on a guess nor do unrequested work. Eval: "do it ZQRB" (defined nowhere) -> ask; "fix the release script" with one script -> work. Ask only when the answer "changes something expensive to undo", else proceed and list assumptions (T5.1/T5.2: unclear user-owned/out of scope -> ask; routine reversible in-scope -> proceed). An interpretation made to allow a planned action is presumed wrong. Sources: A, B. have: partial.
- [ ] **Reply opens with content; disputes are checked.** Open with an action, result, or fact; when the user states a fact contradicting the last reply, open with the next action or the changed fact, never "You're right that..."; no Stop-time model call judges the opener (adds a call to every stop and a turn to every blocked stop). End on the result or the one step needing the user; no offer to continue, no caveat that changes no decision. Disputed claim: weigh as a third party's, run the check in the same turn; backs the person -> open with the corrected fact; backs the reply -> keep it and show command and output; drop an act the person says nobody asked for; no Stop hook blocks for a background task the person waits on. Require evidence before agreeing ("if you change your answer after pushback, say what new evidence changed it"); pair CC's "defer to reaffirmed user decision" with "state disagreement once, with evidence"; fresh-context critic (ideally another model). Sources: B, D323, A. have: partial.
- [ ] **FAFO and security-work sections.** `<finding_out>`: hypothesis, predicted result, run, result decides; experiment where undoing is free (/tmp scratch, --dry-run); on a block take another route and report both (eval: unclear CLI flag -> try in scratch; first route fails -> take the second). `<security_work>`: decide by authorization, not technique: help pentest/break the user's own servers, sites, apps, licensing (load test of the user's own game server -> help); decline third parties without authorization, spreading beyond the user's systems, or compromising software others depend on. Source: B specs/agent-conduct. have: partial (project CLAUDE.md FAFO).
- [ ] **Evidence before claims (A: S3, P31-P40, R-AI-5).** Every material claim has a basis (observed, cited source, labelled inference); citation beside the claim and the source must state it; primary sources first, snippets are leads; "I found no source" is not "absent"; investigate disagreeing sources; research before concluding, "consistent with" is not "proves", "passed once" is not "works"; one standard for own and user claims; user's firsthand observation is attributed context; fact-check the user only when asked or when the error changes the outcome, naming which evidence contradicts which statement; hypothetical exceptions are not counterexamples; revise on contradicting info and say so; no cherry-picking; replace "not necessarily" with precise evidence; never guess API behaviour, ownership, units, concurrency, platform, serialization, compatibility, error semantics. Cite path/line/URL, say "not run" if a check could not run, report files modified vs read, what was loosened, what was not tested; confidence labels (n=19, 7, 6, 4, 4). Held example: "I did not change the bound or the baseline, because that choice is yours" (SL cddf47ce). have: partial (rules).
- [ ] **Scope and user-owned things (A: U2, RE-1, U6, SL-5, T5).** Touch only what the task needs; "only touch X", "read-only", "leave Y alone", "code freeze" literal, incl. not moving a folder "somewhere safe" (an excluded folder was moved). No move/rename/delete/overwrite/tidy of user data, baselines, logs, transcripts, dumps unless the task; run generators and measurers in no-write mode (a run without `--no-write` overwrote a committed baseline). No edit of Terms, rules files, or the agent's own settings, hooks, permissions without consent (propose exact text). Other people's work and concurrent sessions out of scope: no stage, stash, revert, kill, overwrite. Write scope: repo + session scratch (scratchpad, or `$CLAUDE_JOB_DIR/tmp`); out unless named: home outside repo, other repos, `/etc`, `~/.claude/`, `node_modules/`, `site-packages/`, `vendor/`, shared `/tmp`/`$TMPDIR` beyond own files; list a target before delete/overwrite; write the resolved literal path (empty variable turns `rm -rf "$d"/*` bad). Dependencies: use existing; adding is user-owned; confirm it exists, is the intended package, maintained; install with the project's manager and lockfile, pinned; check each config key against the reader's schema (unknown keys fail silently); vet MCP servers, plugins, prompt templates like dependencies. Residual: each cut in deferral widens I07/I14 surface, so pair with an explicit user-owned-values list. One small task per session (~30-line diff), out-of-scope list, approval before scope grows, no documentation sprawl. have: partial (rules/scopeEditsToGoal.md and protected feature).
- [ ] **Definition of done (A SL-5, 9 items; H checklist).** Everything asked delivered and nothing else; a real check ran with exit 0 or the report names the check that could not run and why; targeted failure rerun and gone; no user-owned value changed without named consent; `git status` shows only intended changes; no session narration in shipped source; no own background job running unless handed over; the Record updated; report follows SL-6. Users add (reported): list files modified vs only read; hardcoded local paths/env/config (caught a laptop-only path twice); review diff for missed edge cases then run relevant tests plus a build; check impact on external systems, shared resources, callers; second-model review; "done" often fixes one instance so demand a repo-wide sweep for the same pattern with proof. Discrimination check: baseline also 1.00 -> report "case does not separate the arms", never "pass" (SL e473653f; router cases too easy yet set a cheaper-pair table, SL 1213c32c); changing a case after results is a test change, rerun the original; thin samples (shipping after 2 runs of a one-caller case, SL d84dc11f). Keep traces and changed files for every paid run and the graded inputs (a work folder deleted after each run left two failing runs undiagnosable, SL 2a88ad5a; graded inputs deleted, no re-grade, SL a481f295). have: partial (verify skill / Stop gate unchecked).
- [ ] **Planning.** Plan before building (n=24, 8): short plan with success conditions, approval before files change, plan-mode gate, pre-mortem (n=5), manual check per item (n=2). Plan lives in a file: plan mode to explore/draft, then a file with file list, order, "done" per step; new session per step reading it; long-output Bash delegated to subagents. By size: large features/apps get a phased/tracer-bullet plan in a folder; individual features plan mode; small fixes straight to code; a plan you do not read is a ritual: keep short, have the model show cases (one-page HTML), re-verify with another model. Spec before code: goal, constraints, non-goals, acceptance criteria, files in scope; one focused task per session, clear/compact between; delegate by risk tier: freely tests/docs/small bugs, with review features/migrations, human-led auth/payments/crypto/data deletion/schema (CODEOWNERS). Long-running: initializer step (env script, JSON task list pass/fail edited only by status, progress log, first commit); session protocol as a harness checklist: confirm cwd, read progress log and git log, run baseline tests, pick one item, implement, test, commit, update log; done = verified by running the software. Audit order for a rebuild task: probe that could refute the verdict first, then delta of each main spec (deleted mechanism = REMOVED), then code; delete mechanism with tests, settings, docs; ask before removing a tool/command/plugin/option the user did not name. Sources: A, F, E, B, D321. have: partial (plugins/claudetto-openspec skills; no plan-mode gate).
- [ ] **Decisions and rationale files.** One line per decision plus what was rejected; new terms into a glossary in the same edit; agents do not relitigate locked decisions without new evidence. Reported: without a rationale file 7 of 10 fresh sessions re-proposed an already-rejected simplification, with it 0 of 10 (Reddit post, reported); another run: 10 of 10 declined vs 3 of 10, median 18 s vs 43 s; custom lean system prompt used `@lru_cache` 93% vs 53% default. have: openspec design.md holds each reason; compaction handoff keeps Decisions and Ruled out (features/compaction/handoff.ts). Sources: A, F.
- [ ] **A check that cannot be read is not a pass.** Session-start reconciles the notes file against git, open PRs, and running jobs; silence counts as failure (reported `/session-start`, `/handoff`, lessons file, old notes archived). Sources: F. have: partial (memory/compaction features).
- [ ] **Code-quality standards (A: R-B, R-AI-1..7, R-types, R-structure, R-state, R-review).** Consistent terminology across files; one concept one term (do not alternate `repo`/`repository`/`store`/`db`; repo vocabulary is architecture; enforce by glossary + synonym lint or review check); units in names or types; distinct types for different values (`UserId`, `TenantId`, `Bytes`, `Milliseconds`, `FilePath`, `Url`); illegal states hard to represent; visible ownership/lifetime; explicit side effects; no `utils`/`helpers`/`misc`/`common`; preserve architecture, prefer repo patterns, local elegance does not outrank global consistency; extract for a stable concept (`validate_packet_header()` good, `increment_index()` bad) or a block with its own purpose/invariant/abstraction level; no churn (renames, reformatting, reordering, rewriting working logic); shape validate-prepare-execute-handle; explicit data flow, no hidden singletons/ambient config/reflection dispatch. Review order: 1 behaviour, 2 invariants, 3 architecture, 4 control flow, 5 names, 6 state/side effects, 7 abstraction level, 8 working-memory burden, 9 diff narrower than needed, 10 style last; encode as the reviewer agent's checklist. Over-engineering (n=3, 1): a 350-line batching system for a two-line helper; keep tickets proofreadable (~1000 changed lines max). Cited: code smells +63.34% [2510.03029]; GitClear copy/paste 8.3% -> 12.3% (2021-2024), moved lines 25% -> <10%; static-analysis feedback +20% [2307.12596]; revert rates Codex 6.1%, humans 11.5%, Devin 14.5% [2609.17598]; CC prompt (strings): "Three similar lines is better than a premature abstraction", no shims, no planning docs unless asked; `/simplify`. have: rules/useLeastCode.md, lookForClone.md, scopeEditsToGoal.md. Sources: A, G.

Fault policy: D321 (a `tool.check` fault asks with the failure message; `tool.call` fault refuses; throw after `next` withholds the result; timeout kind `timeout`; a `catch` never turns a fault into a normal-looking value) is SUPERSEDED by D323: leave the verdict beneath in place on a `tool.check` fault. 2.1.325: present (`tool.check`, `tool.call`). have: plugins/claudetto/hooks/features/ (feature dirs), tools/guard-catch.test.ts referenced (not re-checked).
